Title: Confessio, sive Declaratio, Sententiae Pastorum, qui in Foederato Belgio Remonstrantes vocantur, super praecipuis Articulis Religionis Christianae
Creator: The Remonstrant ministers (Remonstrantse Broederschap, as the host record has it); the confession of 1621 was written by Simon Episcopius (see the English file)
Publisher: London: at the expense of Jacob Knapton, at the sign of the Crown in St. Paul's Churchyard (imprint as the OCR reads it; the title page also says it is printed again for students of theology)
Publication Date: 1702 (title page, read in the file)
Rights: Public Domain (published 1702; public domain by date)
Language: lat
Source: Internet Archive item bim_eighteenth-century_confessio-sive-declarat_remonstrantse-broedersch_1702, file bim_eighteenth-century_confessio-sive-declarat_remonstrantse-broedersch_1702_djvu.txt, fetched directly (curl, raw bytes) by the library/source-research thread (Era VII session, one-world agent for the-remonstrants-and-dort), 2026-10-23; host rights statement: none on the item (no possible-copyright-status and no licence field; a microfilm scan contributed by Internet Archive, not access-restricted); collections pub_eighteenth-century, bim_microfilm.
Provenance note: Header prepended; the OCR text follows unchanged apart from long-s to s normalisation (8749 occurrences). Rights basis restated: published 1702; public domain by date.
Content note: The Latin of the Remonstrants' confession, in a London reprint of 1702, after the window. The first Latin printing and the Dutch original of 1621 were not found in a usable scan (Dutch 1621: belijdenisseofte00epis, black-letter OCR unreadable). Chapters headed "CAP." in the OCR; the English translation of 1676 (remonstrants_confession-or-declaration_anon1676.txt) follows the same chapter order. Microfilm OCR with long s: 8749 occurrences of the long-s glyph normalised to s. OCR sampled at 10/30/50/70/90 percent; the body reads as Latin throughout in the samples (about 0.73 of tokens matched a Latin word list, against 0.83 for a clean Latin edition from the same list), but the title-page block and some pages are heavily garbled and marginal marks intrude. SECOND WITNESS for exact wording: verify against the page images before quoting.
